\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma(t)}
as they appear in the above bound.

First, let
\big(\tau_{0,j},B_{j}\big)
,
j=1,\dots,J
, denote the charts introduced in Definition
1.1
corresponding to the initial interface
\Sigma_{0}
. Since
\Sigma(t)
is a free surface, it is covered by the family of charts
\big(\tau_{j}(t),B_{j}\big)
, where

\tau_{j}(t,s)=\tau_{0,j}(s)+\int_{0}^{t}u\big(t^{\prime},\tau_{j}(t^{\prime},s)\big)dt^{\prime}.

From this, it follows that

\left\lVert\nabla\tau_{j}(t)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leqslant\left\lVert\nabla\tau_{0,j}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}e^{\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}},\quad\left\lvert\Sigma(t)\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}\geqslant\left\lvert\Sigma_{0}\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}

and

\left\lvert\nabla\tau_{j}(t)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}\leqslant\left\lvert\nabla\tau_{0,j}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}+e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert\nabla\tau_{j}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}\Big).

We conclude that

\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma(t)}\leqslant\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma_{0}}e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}

and

\big|\Sigma(t)\big|_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}\leqslant\big|\Sigma_{0}\big|_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}+e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big|\Sigma\big|_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}\Big).

On the other hand, we have

D(t)=\big\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\colon\varphi(t,x)>0\big\},\text{ where }\varphi(t)=\varphi_{0}\circ\mathcal{X}^{-1}(t).

It is not difficult to prove

\left\lvert\nabla\varphi(t)\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}\geqslant\left\lvert\nabla\varphi_{0}\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}},

and

\left\lvert\nabla\varphi(t)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}\leqslant\left\lvert\nabla\varphi_{0}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}+e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert\nabla\varphi\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}\Big).

Finally, we come back to (
3.10
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\check{\vartheta}(t)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg(\big|\Sigma_{0}\big|_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}+\left\lvert\nabla\varphi_{0}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}+\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3/(1-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)

(3.11)

\displaystyle+C_{*}e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\psi}\Big(\left\lvert f(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert\nabla\varphi\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}+\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}\Big),

where

\check{\psi}=K_{0}\Big(\psi+\left\lVert\nabla\mathbb{P}u,\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big),\;\text{ and }\;K_{0}=1+\left\lvert\nabla\varphi_{0}\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}^{-1}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma_{0}}.

Grönwall’s Lemma yields

\displaystyle\check{\vartheta}(t)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\left\lvert\Sigma_{0}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}+\left\lvert\nabla\varphi_{0}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}+\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3/(1-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\exp\Bigg(C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}e^{C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\psi}(s)ds\Bigg).

Estimate (
2.12
) finally follows from the fact that

(3.12)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\psi(s)ds\leqslant C_{*}\vartheta(t)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{1/2}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{1/2}.

This concludes the first part of the proof of Lemma
2.1
.
∎

Proof of (

⋄

‣
2.1
).

In this second part of the proof of Lemma
2.1
, we aim to obtain a Lipschitz estimate for the velocity field. To this end, we proceed by estimating each term appearing in (
3.1
). For clarity, our estimates will be expressed in terms of the functional
\phi_{\Sigma}
and
\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}
defined in (
2.10
) above.

\diamond

First, second and fourth terms
: Similarly to (
3.12
), we apply the Gagliardo–Nirenberg’s, Sobolev’s and Young’s inequalities to obtain:

(3.13)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\mu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big).

Next, we express the second term in (
3.1
) as follows:

\displaystyle\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\bigg(\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\Big[\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla,\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big](-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u}).

Estimate (
3.13
) also applies to the last term above. For the remaining term, we use the commutator estimate (
2
) with
\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha/2
,
p=r\in(6/\alpha,\infty)
, and we obtain:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\Big[\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla,\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big](-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lvert\nu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha/2}\left\lVert\nu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\nu}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.14)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big).

We also apply (
2
) to estimate the second-to-last term in (
3.1
) as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\mu(\rho,c)}\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.15)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

\diamond

Remaining terms
:
We express the third and last terms in (
3.1
) as follows:

\displaystyle-\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=-\Big[\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla,\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big]\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big)

(3.16)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big).

Similarly as in the preceding step, we apply (
2
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\Big[\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla,\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big]\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)+\frac{C\mu^{*}}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\vartheta(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Applying (
A
) with
\beta=\alpha/2
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.17)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},

and following the computations leading to (
3.7
), we arrive at:

\displaystyle\left\lvert f(\rho(t),c(t))\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(t)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert G(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg(\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert F(\tau)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg(\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\big(\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+C\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.18)

\displaystyle+C\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Gathering all the above estimates, we deduce the following bound for the first term in the expression (

⋄

‣
3.1
):

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\Big[\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla,\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big]\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C\mu^{*}}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\vartheta(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C\overline{a}}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg\{\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.19)

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg\}.

For the remaining term of (

⋄

‣
3.1
), we shall write

w:=G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)=w^{e}+(w-w^{e}),

where
w^{e}
denotes a
\mathscr{C}^{\alpha}
-extension of
w
that verifies (
A.26
)-(
A.27
)-(
A.28
)-(
A.29
). With the help of (
A.44
), we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\big(w-w^{e}\big)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{3}{p}}\left\lVert w-w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert w-w^{e}\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

(3.20)

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}+\left\lvert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big).

Then, we use the logarithmic interpolation inequality

(3.21)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\left\lVert w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg(1+\log^{+}\Bigg(\frac{\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}{\left\lVert w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg)\Bigg)

and we obtain (with the help of (
A.26
)-(
A.29
))

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg(1+\log^{+}\Bigg(\frac{\left\lvert w\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha/2}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}}{\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg)\Bigg).

Following the computations that lead to (
3.7
), we arrive at (here
\underline{a}_{*}=\min_{c}a_{*}(c)
)

\displaystyle\left\lvert G(\rho,c)(t)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(t)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg(e^{-\underline{a}_{*}t}\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\underline{a}_{*}(t-s)}\left\lvert F(s)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(s)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}ds\bigg),

and (

⋄

‣
3.1
) implies

\displaystyle\left\lvert w(t)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(t)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg\{e^{-\underline{a}_{*}t}\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\underline{a}_{*}(t-s)}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)(t)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(t)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\underline{a}_{*}(t-s)}\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)(s)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(s)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}ds\Bigg\}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert w(s)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg\{1+\int_{0}^{s}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\log^{+}\Bigg[\frac{C_{*}}{\left\lVert w(s)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg(e^{-\underline{a}_{*}s}\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma(s)}^{-\alpha/2}\left\lVert\llbracket w(s)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma(s))}+\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\underline{a}_{*}(s-s^{\prime})}\left\lVert\dot{u}(s^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)(s)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma(s)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\underline{a}_{*}(s-s^{\prime})}\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s^{\prime})ds^{\prime}\Bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg\}.

Next, using the elementary bound
\log^{+}(x)\leqslant\frac{2}{e}\sqrt{x}
, Hölder’s inequality and (

⋄

‣
3.1
) we infer that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla w^{e}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big(1+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,s),L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\big)\Big)ds

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\bigg(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha/2}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}+\left\lvert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\bigg)\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big(1+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,s),L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\big)\Big)ds

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert w\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg\{\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha/2}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}+\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\vartheta(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)

(3.22)

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg\}^{1/2}.

We will now estimate the
L^{\infty}
-bound of
w
and
\llbracket w\rrbracket
appearing in the above estimate and (
3.20
). To achieve this, we go back to (
3.4
) and we write

(3.23)

f\big(\varrho(t,y),c_{0}(y)\big)=e^{-\int_{0}^{t}\check{g}(t^{\prime},y)dt^{\prime}}f\big(\rho_{0}(y),c_{0}(y)\big)-\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\int_{t^{\prime}}^{t}\check{g}(\tau,y)d\tau}F\big(t^{\prime},\mathcal{X}(t^{\prime},y)\big)dt^{\prime},

where (from (
2.8
))

\check{g}(t,y)=\frac{G\big(\varrho(t,y),c_{0}(y)\big)}{f\big(\varrho(t,y),c_{0}(y)\big)}\in\big[a_{*}(c_{0}(y)),a^{*}(c_{0}(y))\big].

We can now readily deduce that

\displaystyle\left\lVert f(\rho,c)(t)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant\overline{a}\left\lVert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\overline{a}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert F(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}.

Recalling the expression of
F
in (
3.8
) and applying the Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s inequality together
with (
3.15
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert f(\rho,c)(t)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant\overline{a}\left\lVert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\overline{a}\int_{0}^{t}\big(\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

(3.24)

\displaystyle+C\overline{a}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Next, we take the jump across the discontinuity surface in (
2.5
) and we obtain:

(3.25)

\displaystyle\left\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-G(\rho,c)\right\rrbracket=\Big\llbracket\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big\rrbracket.

We observe that the balance of forces at the discontinuity surface suggests that the normal component of the stress tensor is continuous, namely:

(3.26)

\displaystyle\llbracket 2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}u\cdot n_{x}+\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u-G(\rho,c)\big)n_{x}\rrbracket=0.

where
n_{x}
denotes the outward normal vector of the surface. Scalar product with
n_{x}
leads to:

(3.27)

\displaystyle\llbracket 2\mu(\rho,c)n_{x}\cdot\mathbb{D}u\cdot n_{x}+\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u-G(\rho,c)\rrbracket=0.

Let
\tau_{x}
and
\overline{\tau}_{x}
be two orthonormal vector fields spanning the tangent plane to
\Sigma
, which can be obtained from
n_{x}\times\vec{e}_{j}
, where
\vec{e}_{j}
,
j=1,2,3
are the vectors of the canonical basis of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then we have (here we use the notation
\partial_{w}g=w\cdot\nabla g
):

n_{x}\cdot\mathbb{D}u\cdot n_{x}=n_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u=\dvg u-\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u-\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u.

Hence (
3.27
) implies

(3.28)

\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-G(\rho,c)\rrbracket=2\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\big(\tau\cdot\partial_{\tau}u+\overline{\tau}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}}u\big),

and consequently (see (
3.25
))

\llbracket[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)\rrbracket=2\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\big(\tau\cdot\partial_{\tau}u+\overline{\tau}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}}u\big).

It immediately follows that for all
q\in[1,\infty]
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\llbracket[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{q}(\Sigma)}\leqslant C\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{q}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

From (
1.16
), we readily obtain

(3.29)

\displaystyle\left\lvert n_{x},\,\tau_{x},\,\overline{\tau}_{x}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}\leqslant C\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha},

and therefore

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\Sigma)}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\beta}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Gathering (
3.19
)-(
3.20
)-(
3.22
)-(
3.24
) together with the computations above, we infer:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\Bigg(\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\Big(G(\rho,c)+\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)\Big)\Bigg)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert w(s)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\vartheta(s)ds

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(1+e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\Bigg\{\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg\}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left\lVert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}+\left\lvert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\check{\vartheta}(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big(1+e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\vartheta(t)\Bigg(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}\Bigg)

(3.30)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\vartheta(t)\Bigg(\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big(\mu^{*}+\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\Bigg).

We have thus completed the estimate for the third and last terms of (
3.1
).

Gathering (
3.30
)-(
3.13
)-(
3.14
)-(
3.15
) we deduce the bound below:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](\mathbb{D}u),\,\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert w(s)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\vartheta(s)ds

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(1+e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\Bigg\{\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg\}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left\lVert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}+\left\lvert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\check{\vartheta}(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big(1+e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\vartheta(t)\Bigg(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}\Bigg)

(3.31)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\vartheta(t)\Bigg(\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big(\mu^{*}+\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\Bigg).

By applying the Leray projector
\mathbb{P}
to (
3.1
) and repeating the same computations as above, we recover estimate (
3.31
) for
\nabla\mathbb{P}u
, so that we can include
\lVert\nabla\mathbb{P}u)\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}
on the LHS of (
3.31
). Then,
we use the smallness of

\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\Bigg[\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}+\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big(\mu^{*}+\overline{a}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\Bigg]

to absorb the last term above into the LHS. Finally, we apply Grönwall’s Lemma to obtain

\displaystyle\vartheta(t)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg\{\left\lVert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{\Sigma}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\Big(1+\delta(t)e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\Big)\Bigg[\left\lvert f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\Big(\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\delta(t)\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\check{\vartheta}(t)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big(1+\delta(t)e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}\Big)+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left\lVert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}+\left\lvert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}(\Sigma)}\Big)

\displaystyle+\vartheta(t)\delta(t)\Bigg(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}e^{\alpha\vartheta(t)}+\big(\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha/2}}\big)\Bigg)\Bigg\}e^{C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\lVert w\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}},

where

\delta(t)=\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c),\,\nu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{p}(\Sigma)}.

The estimate (

⋄

‣
2.1
) follows upon recalling (
3.24
).
∎

3.2.
Proof of Lemma
2.2

Proof.

We first recall that the
(\rho,c)
-dependent function
f=f(\rho,c)
satisfies

\partial_{t}f(\rho,c)+u\cdot\nabla f(\rho,c)+G(\rho,c)=-F.

We write this equation along the flow map of the velocity and we then take jump at the discontinuity surface to obtain:

\partial_{t}\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket+\llbracket G(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket=-\llbracket F\circ\mathcal{X}\rrbracket.

Here,
\mathcal{X}
denotes the flow map of the fluid velocity and
\varrho=\rho\circ\mathcal{X}
. The expression (
3.28
) then implies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket+\llbracket G(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket=-2\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\Big(\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u\Big)\circ\mathcal{X}.

Under the assumption (
1.6
), we write the pressure jump in term of the jump of
f(\rho,c)
, and we obtain

(3.32)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket+g_{P}\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket=-2\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\big(\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u\big)\circ\mathcal{X},

where

g_{P}=\frac{\llbracket\Psi_{P}\big(f(\varrho,c_{0})\big)\rrbracket}{\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket}=\int_{0}^{1}\Psi_{P}^{\prime}\big(rf(\varrho^{+},1)+(1-r)f(\varrho^{-},0)\big)dr.

From (
1.8
)-(
2.8
), we have
g_{P}\in[a_{*},a^{*}]\subset(0,\infty)
; and it follows that

(3.33)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho(t),c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leqslant e^{-a_{*}t}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\varrho(t^{\prime}),c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}.

From this, it is straightforward to deduce (
2.15
).

Taking difference in (
3.32
), we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}+g_{P}(s_{2})\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}

\displaystyle=-2\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\big(\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u\big)\circ\mathcal{X}\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}

\displaystyle-\big(g_{P}(s_{2})-g_{P}(s_{1})\big)\llbracket f(\varrho(s_{1}),c_{0})\rrbracket,

and whence,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho(t),c_{0})\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leqslant e^{-a_{*}t}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\right\rvert+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})}\left\lvert g_{P}(s_{2})-g_{P}(s_{1})\right\rvert\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})(s_{1})\rrbracket\right\rvert dt^{\prime}

(3.34)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\big(\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u\big)\circ\mathcal{X}\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\right\rvert dt^{\prime}.

Since we have

\displaystyle g_{P}(s_{2})-g_{P}(s_{1})

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\Big(rf\big(\varrho^{+}(s),1\big)\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}+(1-r)f\big(\varrho^{-}(s),0\big)\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}\Big)\Psi_{P}^{\prime\prime}\big(r^{\prime}\big(rf(\varrho^{+}(s_{2}),1)+(1-r)f(\varrho^{-}(s_{2}),0)\big)

(3.35)

\displaystyle+(1-r^{\prime})\big(rf(\varrho^{+}(s_{1}),1)+(1-r)f(\varrho^{-}(s_{1}),0)\big)\big)drdr^{\prime},

it is not difficult to derive (we use (
3.29
))

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho,c)(t)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma(t))}

\displaystyle\leqslant e^{-a_{*}t+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma_{0})}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert f(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime},

and hence

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\llbracket G(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{a^{*}}{a_{*}}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\Bigg[\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma_{0})}+\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lvert f(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg].

This concludes the proof of (
2.16
).

Under the assumption (
1.9
), we have

\left\lvert\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert\leqslant C_{*}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert,

and by applying Grönwall’s lemma to (
3.33
), we find:

(3.36)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho(t),c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leqslant e^{-a_{*}t+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert.

It is then straightforward to deduce (
2.17
).

To prove (

⋄

‣
2.2
), we express

\llbracket\mu(\varrho,c_{0})(s)\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}=g_{\mu}(s_{2})\llbracket f\big(\varrho,c_{0}\big)(s)\rrbracket\Big|_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}+\big(g_{\mu}(s_{2})-g_{\mu}(s_{1})\big)\llbracket f(\varrho,c_{0})(s_{1})\rrbracket,

where

g_{\mu}=\int_{0}^{1}\Psi_{\mu}^{\prime}\big(rf(\varrho^{+},1)+(1-r)f(\varrho^{-},0)\big)dr.

Hence, proceeding as in (
3.35
), we find that (
3.34
) implies:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho,c)(t)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\leqslant e^{-a_{*}t+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma_{0})}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lVert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-a_{*}(t-t^{\prime})+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}\left\lvert f(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\llbracket f(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\big(1+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)dt^{\prime}.

Whence, Grönwall’s lemma and (
3.36
) yields

\displaystyle\left\lvert\llbracket f(\rho,c)(t)\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma(t))}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}e^{-a_{*}t+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}e^{\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}}\Bigg[\left\lvert\llbracket f(\varrho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma_{0})}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\llbracket f(\rho_{0},c_{0})\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}+\left\lvert f(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\Sigma)}\big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert f(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg].

This proves (

⋄

‣
2.2
) and we now turn to the decay estimate for the velocity gradient jump.

To this end, we first express

\nabla u^{j}=\partial_{\tau_{x}}u^{j}\tau_{x}+\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u^{j}\overline{\tau}_{x}+\partial_{n_{x}}u^{j}n_{x},

and since
\partial_{\tau_{x}}u^{j}
and
\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u^{j}
are continuous across
\Sigma
, we have

(3.37)

\displaystyle\llbracket\nabla u^{j}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket\partial_{n_{x}}u^{j}\rrbracket n_{x}.

From (
3.27
), we have

\llbracket 2\mu(\rho,c)n_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u+\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u-P(\rho,c)\rrbracket=0,

and since

\dvg u=\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u+n_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u,

we deduce that

\llbracket\nu(\rho,c)n_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u\rrbracket=\llbracket P(\rho,c)\rrbracket-\llbracket\lambda(\rho,c)\rrbracket\big(\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u\big).

and hence

n_{x}\cdot\llbracket\partial_{n_{x}}u\rrbracket=\frac{1}{\left\langle\nu(\rho,c)\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}\llbracket P(\rho,c)\rrbracket-\llbracket\lambda(\rho,c)\rrbracket\frac{\left\langle\dvg u\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}{\left\langle\nu(\rho,c)\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}-2\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\frac{n_{x}\cdot\left\langle\partial_{n_{x}}u\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}{\left\langle\nu(\rho,c)\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}.

Similarly, scalar product of (
3.26
) with the tangential vector fields
\tau_{x}
and
\overline{\tau}_{x}
yields:

\displaystyle\tau_{x}\cdot\llbracket\partial_{n_{x}}u\rrbracket=-\frac{\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket}{\left\langle\mu(\rho,c)\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}\big(n_{x}\cdot\partial_{\tau_{x}}u+\tau_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u\big),

and

\displaystyle\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\llbracket\partial_{n_{x}}u\rrbracket=-\frac{\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket}{\left\langle\mu(\rho,c)\right\rangle_{\text{avg}}}\big(n_{x}\cdot\partial_{\overline{\tau}_{x}}u+\overline{\tau}_{x}\cdot\partial_{n_{x}}u\big),

Estimate (
2.19
) simply follows from (
3.37
) and these expressions. This completes the proof of Lemma
2.2
.
∎

3.3.
Basic a priori estimates

In this subsection, we derive some basic a priori bounds for the solutions to the system (
2.1
).
These estimates consist of mass conservation and energy balance, and follow from the renormalized form of the equations
for the volume fraction and the density (see
\eqref{ep2.1}_{1,2}
):

(3.38)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}b(\rho,c)+\dvg\big(b(\rho,c)u\big)+\big(\rho\partial_{\rho}b(\rho,c)-b(\rho,c)\big)\dvg u=0.

Above,
b
is any
W^{1,\infty}_{\text{loc}}
-regular function of the density and volume fraction.

Conservation of mass.
Let
\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}(c)
be a
W^{1,\infty}_{\text{loc}}
-regular function. We observe that

b(\rho,c)=\rho\mathcal{H}(c)
satisfies

\rho\partial_{\rho}b(\rho,c)-b(\rho,c)=0,

and (
3.38
) becomes

\partial_{t}\big(\rho\mathcal{H}(c)\big)+\dvg\big(\rho\mathcal{H}(c)u\big)=0.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho(t,x)\mathcal{H}(c(t,x))dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho_{0}(x)\mathcal{H}(c_{0}(x))dx,

as soon as
\rho_{0}\mathcal{H}(c_{0})\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
. This bound, as well as those in the following paragraph, holds true even when the volume fraction is not an indicator function.

Energy balance.
Here, we consider
b
as solution of the ODE

(3.39)

\displaystyle\rho\partial_{\rho}b(\rho,c)-b(\rho,c)=P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}.

Since this is a first-order ODE, an additional condition is required to uniquely determine
b
.
In order to work within a framework that allows for densities close to two constants (
\widetilde{\rho}(1)
in
D^{c}(t)
, and
\widetilde{\rho}(0)
in
D(t)
) we shall impose the following condition:

b(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=0.

Such a function
b
, now denoted by
H_{1}(\rho,c))
, is therefore given by

H_{1}(\rho,c)=\rho\int_{\widetilde{\rho}(c)}^{\rho}\dfrac{1}{\varrho^{2}}\Big(P(\varrho,c)-\widetilde{P}\Big)d\varrho.

Moreover, in view of (
3.38
), it satisfies the equation

\partial_{t}H_{1}(\rho,c)+\dvg\big(H_{1}(\rho,c)u\big)+\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\dvg u=0,

and consequently

(3.40)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c)=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\dvg u.

Differentiating (
3.39
) with respect to the density, and using the fact that the map
\rho\mapsto P(\rho,c)
is non-decreasing, we deduce that
\rho\mapsto H_{1}(\rho,c)
is convex. The non-negativity of
(\rho,c)\mapsto H_{1}(\rho,c)
then follows from
H_{1}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=\partial_{\rho}H_{1}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=0
.

As is standard, to compute the classical energy balance, we use the velocity field
u
as a test function in
\eqref{ep2.1}_{3}
and we obtain

(3.41)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\dfrac{\left\lvert u\right\rvert^{2}}{2}-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dvg u\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}u^{j}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)=0.

We have already computed the second term above in (
3.40
), and now, using the symmetry of
\mathbb{D}u
we have

\sum_{j,k}\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}u^{j}=\sum_{j,k}\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{j}u^{k}\implies\sum_{j,k}\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}u^{j}=\dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{j,k}\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\big(\partial_{k}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{k}\big)=\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2},

and therefore (
3.41
) leads to

(3.42)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}E+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)=0,

where the energy functional
E
is given by

E(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\bigg(\rho\dfrac{\left\lvert u\right\rvert^{2}}{2}+H_{1}\big(\rho,c\big)\bigg)(t,x)dx,\text{ and we set }E(0)=E_{0}.

The term contributing to the energy potential in (
3.41
) can be computed as follows:

-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\dvg u=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(P(\rho,c)-c\widetilde{P})\dvg u-\widetilde{P}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(c-1)\dvg u.

From the equation on the volume fraction (see
\eqref{ep2.1}_{1}
), we express the last term above as:

-\widetilde{P}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(c-1)\dvg u=\widetilde{P}\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1-c),

and thanks to (
3.38
), the previous one is

-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(P(\rho,c)-c\widetilde{P})\dvg u=\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\check{H}_{1}(\rho,c),\text{ where }\check{H}_{1}(\rho,c)=\rho\int_{c\widetilde{\rho}}^{\rho}\dfrac{1}{\varrho^{2}}\Big(P(\varrho,c)-c\widetilde{P}\Big)d\varrho\geqslant 0.

As a result, we have

(3.43)

\displaystyle\check{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)=\check{E}(0)=:\check{E}_{0},

where

\check{E}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\bigg(\rho\dfrac{\left\lvert u\right\rvert^{2}}{2}+\check{H}_{1}\big(\rho,c\big)+\widetilde{P}\big(1-c\big)\bigg)(t,x)dx.

All the computations above remain valid even when the volume fraction is not an indicator function. In the indicator-function setting, we have
1-c(t)=\mathbb{1}_{D(t)}
and (
3.43
) yields

(3.44)

\displaystyle\left\lvert D(t)\right\rvert=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(1-c\big)(t,x)dx\leqslant\widetilde{P}^{-1}\check{E}_{0}.

This provides a sharper estimate of the volume of the moving domain than the one obtained
using the flow of the velocity field.

The potential energies
H_{l}
.
In the spirit of (
3.39
), for
l\in[1,\infty)
we define the
potential energy
H_{l}=H_{l}(\rho,c)
as the solution of

(3.45)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\rho\partial_{\rho}H_{l}(\rho,c)-H_{l}(\rho,c)=\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big),\\
H_{l}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=0.\end{cases}

It reads

H_{l}(\rho,c)=\rho\int_{\widetilde{\rho}(c)}^{\rho}\dfrac{1}{\varrho^{2}}\left\lvert P(\varrho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\varrho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)d\varrho,

and is clearly nonnegative as

\rho\partial^{2}_{\rho}H_{l}(\rho,c)=l\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\geqslant 0,\text{ and }\partial_{\rho}H_{l}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=H_{l}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=0.

In particular, the map
\rho\mapsto H_{l}(\rho,c)
is convex, which leads to the inequality

0=H_{l}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\geqslant H_{l}(\rho,c)+\partial_{\rho}H_{l}(\rho,c)\big(\widetilde{\rho}(c)-\rho\big),

and combined with (
3.45
), this implies

(3.46)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\rho}(c)H_{l}(\rho,c)\leqslant\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big).

For some functions
g=g(\rho,c)
, and
a_{l}=a_{l}(c)
to be specified later, let us consider

g_{l}(\rho,c)=\dfrac{H_{l}(\rho,c)}{\rho}-\dfrac{a_{l}(c)}{\rho}\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)g(\rho,c),

and compute

\displaystyle\rho^{2}\partial_{\rho}g_{l}(\rho,c)=\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}-a_{l}(c)\rho\partial_{\rho}g(\rho,c)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)

\displaystyle-a_{l}(c)g(\rho,c)\bigg(l\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}-\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\Bigg[1-a_{l}(c)\big(\rho\partial_{\rho}g(\rho,c)-g(\rho,c)\big)-la_{l}(c)\dfrac{\rho g(\rho,c)\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)}{P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}}\Bigg].

\diamond

Choosing
g(\rho,c)=\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)
and
a_{l}(c)=1/\widetilde{\rho}(c)
ensures that the expression within the square bracket is non-positive, as the map
\rho\mapsto P(\rho,c)
is non-decreasing. As a result, the map
\rho\mapsto g_{l}(\rho,c)
is non-decreasing on
[0,\widetilde{\rho}(c)]
, non-increasing on
[\widetilde{\rho}(c),\infty)
, with

g_{l}(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)=0
. This implies that
g\leq 0
, which in turn leads us again to (
3.46
).

\diamond

Setting

g(\rho,c)=1-\frac{2\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)},

the expression inside the square bracket above becomes

1-a_{l}(c)\Bigg[1-\Bigg(\frac{\rho}{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)}\Bigg)^{2}+l\frac{\rho}{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)}\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dfrac{\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}}\Bigg].

We now choose
a_{l}(c)>0
such that

a_{l}(c)\Bigg(1+l\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dfrac{\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}}\Bigg)\leqslant 1,

and, following the same reasoning as before, we obtain that
g_{l}\geqslant 0
, which in turn implies

(3.47)

\displaystyle a_{l}(c)\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big)\leqslant\big(\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big)H_{l}(\rho,c).

In general,
a_{l}(c)
depends only on
l
and the upper bound of the density. However, in specific cases, such as when
the pressure law of the phase "+" (resp. "-") is polynomial,
a_{l}(1)
(resp.
a_{l}(0)
, can be chosen independently
of the density’s upper bound.

The potential energies
H_{l}
will serve to derive an estimate for the
L^{l+1}_{t,x}
-norm of the pressure fluctuation. Indeed, from (
3.45
)-(
3.38
), one readily obtains

(3.48)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\dvg u=0.

Next, we express the velocity divergence in terms of the flux
F
given by

(3.49)

\displaystyle F=\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-P(\rho,c)+\widetilde{P},

and apply Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, and we arrive at

(3.50)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)+\dfrac{1}{l+1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dfrac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l+1}\leqslant\dfrac{1}{l+1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dfrac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\left\lvert F\right\rvert^{l+1}.

We have already known (see (
2.5
)) that this flux can be rewritten as

(3.51)

\displaystyle F=-(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})+[(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg,\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u),

and its
L_{t,x}^{l+1}
-bound will follow from energy estimates for the material acceleration
\dot{u}
.

3.4.
Proof of Lemma
2.3

The goal of this section is to derive estimates for the functionals
\mathcal{A}_{1}
,
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, and
\mathcal{A}_{3}
.
The starting point (see Section
3.4.1
) will be to prove that, under the low frequency assumption (
1.14
), we gain a time decay for the classical energy.

3.4.1.
Time-decay of the classical energy

This paragraph is dedicated to proving the following estimate.

Lemma 3.1
.

There exists a constant
c_{*}>0
, depending only on the
lower and upper bounds of the density, such that if
E_{0}\leqslant c_{*}
, then the following holds:

(3.52)

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,t]}\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}E

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}E(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\big)dt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}.

Proof of Lemma
3.1
.

The proof of (
3.52
) proceeds as follows: we establish a uniform-in-time bound on the

L^{2}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
-norm of both the high- and low-frequency components of the velocity and pressure fluctuations. These in turn lead to (
3.52
). Our method is similar to that in
[
25
]
,but with major differences.

We shall introduce the following notations:
\mathscr{F}
will denote the Fourier transform:

\mathscr{F}f(\xi)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f(x)e^{-ix\cdot\xi}dx.

For a smooth real function
\psi
satisfying,
\psi(\xi)=1
for
\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert\leqslant 1/2
, and
\psi(\xi)=0
for
\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert\geqslant 1
, we define the low-frequency part of
f
, denoted
f^{l}
, and high-frequency part of
f
, denoted
f^{h}
, as follows:

f^{l}=\mathscr{F}^{-1}\big(\xi\mapsto\psi(\xi)\mathscr{F}(f)(\xi)\big),\quad f^{h}=f-f^{l}.

Step 1:
L^{2}_{t,x}
-bound for the high-frequency components

By applying the Plancherel–Parseval identity, we obtain:

\displaystyle\lVert u^{h}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(1-\psi(\xi)\big)^{2}\left\lvert\mathscr{F}u(\xi)\right\rvert^{2}d\xi

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert\geqslant 1/2}\left\lvert\mathscr{F}u(\xi)\right\rvert^{2}d\xi

\displaystyle\leqslant 4\int_{\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert\geqslant 1/2}\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert^{2}\left\lvert\mathscr{F}u(\xi)\right\rvert^{2}d\xi

\displaystyle\leqslant 4\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

It immediately follows that (see (
3.42
))

(3.53)

\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert u^{h}(t^{\prime})\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

Next, from the expressions of the effective flux (see (
3.49
)-(
3.51
)), we have

\displaystyle G(\rho,c)=(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})+\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-[(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg,\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)

and it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{h}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\big)\varrho^{h}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-[(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg,\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)\big)\varrho^{h},

where
\varrho
denotes the density fluctuation around its equilibrium states

\varrho=\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big).

We estimate the last term above as:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\nu(\rho,c)\dvg u-[(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg,\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}](2\mathbb{D}u)\big)\varrho^{h}dt^{\prime}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

We express
\rho\dot{u}=\partial_{t}(\rho u)+\dvg(\rho u\otimes u)
, and the remaining term is computed as

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\big)\varrho^{h}=\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\varrho^{h}

(3.54)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\partial_{t}\varrho^{h}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg(\rho u\otimes u)\big)\varrho^{h}.

We bound the last term as:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg(\rho u\otimes u)\big)\varrho^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Observing that
\varrho
solves
\partial_{t}\varrho+\dvg(\varrho u)+\widetilde{\rho}(c)\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\dvg u=0
, writing
\rho=(\rho-\widetilde{\rho})+\widetilde{\rho}
, and applying Hölder and Sobolev’s inequalities, we obtain :

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\partial_{t}\varrho^{h}\Bigg|\leqslant\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\dvg(\varrho u)^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\big(\widetilde{\rho}(c)\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\dvg u\big)^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\big((\rho-\widetilde{\rho})u\big)\big)\dvg(\varrho u)^{h}\Bigg|+\widetilde{\rho}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg u\big)\dvg(\varrho u)^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\big((\rho-\widetilde{\rho})u\big)\big)\big(\widetilde{\rho}(c)\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\dvg u\big)^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\widetilde{\rho}\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg u\big)\big(\widetilde{\rho}(c)\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\dvg u\big)^{h}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\big(1+\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\big(1+\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

The time integral of the remaining term in (
3.54
) is

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big((-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho u)\big)\varrho^{h}dt^{\prime}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}-1}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}.

Summing everything together, we obtain:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{h}

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\varrho\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+C\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}-1}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}+\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\big(1+\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

Recalling that
\widetilde{P}=P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)
, we infer

(3.55)

\displaystyle G(\rho,c)=\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big)\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\rho}P\big(r\rho+(1-r)\widetilde{\rho}(c),c\big)dr,

and using the monotonicity of the map
\rho\mapsto P(\rho,c)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}\leqslant\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{l}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{h}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{l}+C\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}-1}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\sup_{[0,t]}\Big(\frac{1}{\eta}+\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\big(1+\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

From (
3.55
), we estimate the second term of the RHS above as:

\left\lvert\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\varrho^{l}\right\rvert dt^{\prime}\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho^{l}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

Holder’s and Young’s inequalities imply (since
r_{0}\in(0,1)
)

C\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}-1}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\frac{C}{\eta}E_{0}.

Interpolation inequality yields

\displaystyle C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}

\displaystyle\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{1/4}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{3/4}

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}E_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho^{l}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}+C\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

(3.56)

\displaystyle+C_{*}E_{0}+C_{*}\big(1+E_{0}^{2/3}+\sup_{[0,t]}\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big(1+\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

Estimates (
3.4.1
)-(
3.53
) conclude this paragraph, and we will now derive bounds for the low-frequency component of the velocity and density fluctuations.

Step 2:
L^{2}_{t,x}
-bound for the low-frequency components

In order to derive an estimate for the
L^{2}_{t,x}
-bound of the low-frequency component of the velocity and density fluctuations, we first write the following equations from (
2.1
):

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\varrho+\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho},1)\dvg(\rho u)&=\dvg\big(\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho},1)(\rho-\widetilde{\rho})u-\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c))(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c))u\big)\\
&+\big(\widetilde{\rho}\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho},1)-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\big)\dvg u\\
&=:\mathbb{F},\\
\partial_{t}(\rho u)+\nabla\varrho&-\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\Delta(\rho u)-\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}+\widetilde{\lambda}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\nabla\dvg(\rho u)\\
&=-\dvg(\rho u\otimes u)-\nabla\big(P(\rho,c)-\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(c),c)\varrho-\widetilde{P}\big)+\dvg\big(2(\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu})\mathbb{D}u\big)\\
&+\nabla\big((\lambda(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\lambda})\dvg u\big)+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\Delta\big(\widetilde{\rho}-\rho)u\big)+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}+\widetilde{\lambda}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\nabla\dvg\big(\widetilde{\rho}-\rho)u\big)\\
&=:\mathbb{G}.\end{cases}

Defining the differential operator
\mathbb{A}
by

\mathbb{A}=\begin{pmatrix}0&\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho},1)\dvg\\
\nabla&-\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\Delta-\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}+\widetilde{\lambda}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\nabla\dvg\end{pmatrix},

the pair
(\varrho,\rho u)
can then be written as:

(3.57)

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\varrho\\
\rho u\end{pmatrix}(t)=e^{-t\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}\varrho\\
\rho u\end{pmatrix}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\mathbb{(}t-s)\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{F}\\
\mathbb{G}\end{pmatrix}(s)ds.

The semi-group
\big(e^{-t\mathbb{A}}\big)_{t\geqslant 0}
is known (see
[
11
,
33
]
) to satisfy the following property: there exist positive constants
C
and
k_{0}
depending only on the constants coefficients of
\mathbb{A}
, such that:

(3.58)

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathscr{F}\big(e^{-t\mathbb{A}}U\big)(\xi)\right\rvert\leqslant Ce^{-k_{0}t\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert^{2}}\left\lvert\mathscr{F}\big(U\big)(\xi)\right\rvert,\;\text{ for all }\left\lvert\xi\right\rvert\leqslant 1.

It immediately follows that for all
m\in\mathbb{N}
, and
U\in L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, with
q\in[1,2]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\big(e^{-t\mathbb{A}}\nabla^{m}U\big)^{l}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}(\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{m}{3})}\left\lVert U\right\rVert_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},\;\text{ for all }t>0.

As a result, we have the following bounds.

\diamond

With
p_{0}\in(1,6/5)
and
r_{0}\in(0,3/p_{0}-5/2)
, we have
r_{0}-\frac{3}{p_{0}}+\frac{3}{2}<-1
, and we can estimate the first term on the RHS of (
3.57
) as:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert e^{-t^{\prime}\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}\varrho\\
\rho u\end{pmatrix}_{|t=0}^{l}\right\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}-\frac{3}{p_{0}}+\frac{3}{2}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

\diamond

The source term
\mathbb{G}
is expressed as

\mathbb{G}=\mathbb{G}_{1}+\mathbb{G}_{2},\;\text{ where }\;\mathbb{G}_{2}=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\Delta\big(\widetilde{\rho}-\rho)u\big)+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}+\widetilde{\lambda}}{\widetilde{\rho}}\nabla\dvg\big(\widetilde{\rho}-\rho)u\big),

and we estimate

\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}e^{-\mathbb{(}t^{\prime}-s)\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\mathbb{G}_{2}^{l}\end{pmatrix}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}}\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds.

Given that for all
s\in(0,t^{\prime})
, we have
\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\leqslant\left\langle s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}+\left\langle t-s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}e^{-\mathbb{(}t^{\prime}-s)\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\mathbb{G}_{2}^{l}\end{pmatrix}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{[0,t]}\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}}\left\langle s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}((0,t))},

and Young’s inequality for convolution and Sobolev embedding yield:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}e^{-\mathbb{(}t^{\prime}-s)\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\mathbb{G}_{2}^{l}\end{pmatrix}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big).

For the remaining term, we first observe that (see (
3.44
))

\left\lVert\widetilde{\rho}(c(t))-\widetilde{\rho},\;\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho}(t),c(t))-\partial_{\rho}P(\widetilde{\rho},1)\right\rVert_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\left\lvert D(t)\right\rvert^{1/p}\leqslant C\check{E}_{0}^{1/p},

and via Taylor expansion

\left\lVert P(\rho,c)-\varrho-\widetilde{P}\right\rVert_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

It then follows that (recall that
r_{0}<1/2
)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}e^{-\mathbb{(}t^{\prime}-s)\mathbb{A}}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{F}\\
\mathbb{G}_{1}\end{pmatrix}^{l}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}+C_{*}\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}}\left\langle s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\check{E}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}+\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\check{E}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}\left\langle s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\check{E}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\check{E}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\left\lVert\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\left\langle t^{\prime}-s\right\rangle^{-\frac{5}{4}}\left\langle s\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(s)ds\right\rVert_{L^{2}(0,t)}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\big(E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\check{E}_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\Big)+C_{*}\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{4}}\varrho\right\rVert_{L^{4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}.

Summing all these computations, we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\varrho^{l},(\rho u)^{l}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C_{*}E_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\left\lVert\varrho\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

(3.59)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\big(E_{0}+\check{E}_{0}\big)\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\Big).

Applying Hölder’s and Sobolev’s inequalities, we have

\lVert\widetilde{\rho}u^{l}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant\lVert(\rho u)^{l}\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\lVert\rho-\widetilde{\rho}\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Combining these with (
3.53
) and (
3.4.1
), and the smallness of
E_{0}
(to absorb the second term on the RHS of (
3.59
) into the LHS) we derive:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\varrho,u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(E_{0}+\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\Big)
