\displaystyle+C_{*}\big(E_{0}+\check{E}_{0}\big)\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\big(1+E_{0}^{2/3}+\check{E}_{0}^{2/3}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

With this estimate in hand, we multiply (
3.42
) by
\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}
and integrate in time to obtain:

\displaystyle\left\langle t\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}E(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}E(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant E_{0}+C\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}E(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(E_{0}+\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\rho u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lVert\varrho\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\big(E_{0}+\check{E}_{0}\big)\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{1}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\big(1+E_{0}^{2/3}+\check{E}_{0}^{2/3}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

It is straightforward to obtain the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{-2r(1+r_{0})}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}-r}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{r}

\displaystyle\leqslant C(r)E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-r}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{r},\;\text{ with }\;\frac{1}{2(1+r_{0})}<r<\frac{1}{2};

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{\frac{r_{0}}{2}}\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{5/4}((0,t),L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{5/4}

\displaystyle\leqslant C(r)E_{0}^{\frac{5}{8}-\check{r}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u(s)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}ds\Bigg)^{\check{r}},\;\text{ with }\;\frac{5}{8}-\frac{1/4}{1+r_{0}}<\check{r}<\frac{5}{8};

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant E_{0}^{\frac{1}{1+r_{0}}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u(t^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{r_{0}}{1+r_{0}}}.

We choose

\check{r}=\frac{5}{4}r,\;\text{ with }\;\max\Bigg\{1-\frac{r_{0}}{1+r_{0}};1-\frac{2/5}{1+r_{0}}\Bigg\}<2r<1,

and apply Young’s inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,t]}\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}E

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}E(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\big)dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(E_{0}\big(1+E_{0}+\check{E}_{0}\big)+E_{0}^{\frac{r}{1-r}}\big(E_{0}+\check{E}_{0}\big)^{\frac{1}{1-r}}+\left\lVert(\varrho,\rho u)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{p_{0}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big).

This completes the proof of (
3.52
), and we now proceed to the bound of the functionals
\mathcal{A}_{1}
,
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, and
\mathcal{A}_{3}
defined in (
2.20
).
∎

3.4.2.
Bounds for the functional
\mathcal{A}_{1}

This subsection aim to provide a bound for the functional
\mathcal{A}_{1}
defined in (
2.20
).

Proof.

To achieve this, we use the material acceleration
\dot{u}

as a test function in the momentum equation
\eqref{ep2.1}_{3}
and we obtain

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\lambda(\rho,c)\left\lvert\dvg u\right\rvert^{2}\Big]+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)-\mu(\rho,c)\right)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}\dvg u

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{j}-\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)-\lambda(\rho,c)\right)(\dvg u)^{3}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u\nabla u^{k}\partial_{k}u+\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u

(3.60)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)-G(\rho,c))(\dvg u)^{2}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\nabla u^{k}\cdot\partial_{k}u.

We multiply (
3.42
) by a constant
L\geqslant 1
and add it
to the above equality, then multiply the resulting expression by
\kappa_{1}(t)=\left\langle t\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}
before integrating in
time:

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{1}(t)}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)\left\lvert\dvg u\right\rvert^{2}\Big](t,x)dx+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+L\kappa_{1}(t)E(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[2L\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\big(L\lambda(\rho,c)-\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\big)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big](t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=LE_{0}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[\mu(\rho_{0},c_{0})\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u_{0}\right\rvert^{2}+\dfrac{1}{2}\lambda(\rho_{0},c_{0})\left\lvert\dvg u_{0}\right\rvert^{2}\Big]+L\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})E(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\dfrac{1}{2}\lambda(\rho,c)\left\lvert\dvg u\right\rvert^{2}\Big]dt^{\prime}-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho_{0},c_{0})\dvg u_{0}

\displaystyle+\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u-2\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{j}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)-\mu(\rho,c)\right)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}\dvg u-\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)-\lambda(\rho,c)\right)(\dvg u)^{3}

(3.61)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u\nabla u^{k}\partial_{k}u-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\nabla u^{k}\cdot\partial_{k}u.

In view of the classical energy balance (
3.42
) and (
3.52
), the terms appearing in the second and third lines above are all bounded by

C_{*}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u_{0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+LC_{0}\big).

With the help of Hölder and Young’s inequalities the term that follows can be bounded as follows:

\Bigg|\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u\Bigg|\leqslant\frac{\kappa_{1}(t)}{\eta L}\left\lVert\dvg u(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+L\eta\kappa_{1}(t)\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Next, using (
3.40
) we have:

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u

\displaystyle=\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t)H_{1}(\rho(t),c(t))-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime\prime}(t^{\prime})H_{1}(\rho,c)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime},

and hence

\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\dvg u\Bigg|\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}.

All the remaining terms in the RHS of (
3.61
) are bounded by

C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

In sum, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)+L\kappa_{1}(t)E(t)+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[2L\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}u\right\rvert^{2}+\big(L\lambda(\rho,c)-\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\big)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big](t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant L\eta\kappa_{1}(t)\lVert G(\rho,c)\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{\eta L}\kappa_{1}(t)\left\lVert\dvg u(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Bigg(\left\lVert\nabla u_{0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+LC_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}\Bigg).

Given that

\lVert G(\rho,c)\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c),

we first fix
\eta
small to absorb the first RHS term into the left, and then choose
L
large, depending on the shear viscosity lower bound, to ensure that the last LHS term is nonnegative and the second RHS term is absorbed into the left. It then follows that

(3.62)

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\left\lVert\nabla u_{0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\lVert G(\rho,c)\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}\Bigg).

What remains is to estimate the last two terms above.

To achieve this, we recall the expression of the velocity gradient (
2.6
), from which we deduce

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}C_{l}^{1/(l+1)}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

(3.63)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}C_{l}^{1/(l+1)}\Big(\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big).

Next, we recall (
3.50
), from which we deduce:

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{3\mu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho_{0},c_{0})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)dt^{\prime}+\dfrac{1}{3\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert F\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

It then follows that (see the expression of
F
in (
3.51
))

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)(t,x)dx

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{3\mu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\mu_{*}^{3}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho_{0},c_{0})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

We will now estimate the terms appearing in the RHS above.

\diamond

From (
3.46
)-(
3.47
), we have:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\rho}(c)H_{l+1}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lvert P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\right\rvert^{l-1}\big(P(\rho,c)-\widetilde{P}\big)\big(\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)\big)

(3.64)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\frac{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{a_{l}(c)}H_{l}(\rho,c).

In particular, we have (see (
3.52
))

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho_{0},c_{0})+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}.

\diamond

Owing to the expressions (
2.4
)-(
2.5
) and Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s inequality, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{3}{2}}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{3/4}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\bigg(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}\bigg)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}E^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big).

\diamond

Applying (
A.8
) with
p=3
and
\alpha^{\prime}=\frac{\alpha}{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert\mu(\rho,c)\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha/2}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\ell^{-\alpha/2}_{\Sigma}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2+\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+C\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.65)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2+\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big),

and hence

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{3}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2+\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}.

The same estimate holds for

\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}.

Combining all these estimates, we infer

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{3\mu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\mu_{*}^{3}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{*}E^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{3}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2+\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}.

Therefore, if

(3.66)

\displaystyle C\frac{\mu^{*}}{\mu_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Bigg)^{3}\leqslant\frac{1}{6},

the second term on the RHS above can be absorbed into the LHS, and applying interpolation and Young’s inequalities to the last term we obtain:

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

(3.67)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(C_{0}+E^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big)\Big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}.

Returning to (
3.62
), we deduce:

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)+\kappa_{1}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(C_{0}+\left\lVert\nabla u_{0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+E^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big)\Big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}.

We estimate the last term above as

\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}dt^{\prime}\leqslant\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\sup_{[0,t]}\big(\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}

and we apply Young’s inequality to obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)+\sup_{[0,t]}\kappa_{1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{2}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{3}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}E_{0}\big)+\left\lVert\nabla u_{0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+E^{\frac{1}{4}}_{0}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big)\Big).

This completes the proof of (
2.22
).
∎

3.4.3.
Bound for the functional
\mathcal{A}_{2}

This section is devoted to deriving a bound for the functional
\mathcal{A}_{2}
defined in (
2.20
) above.

Proof.

The functional
\mathcal{A}_{2}
arises when applying the material derivative
\partial_{t}\cdot+\dvg(\cdot\,u)
to the momentum equation
\eqref{ep2.1}_{3}
, followed by a testing against
\dot{u}
. This yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\Big)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)-\lambda(\rho,c))(\dvg u)^{2}\dvg\dot{u}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{k}\cdot\partial_{k}u\dvg\dot{u}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg u\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu(\rho,c)\partial_{k}u\cdot\nabla\dot{u}^{j}\mathbb{D}^{jk}u

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu(\rho,c)\big(\partial_{j}u\cdot\nabla u^{k}+\partial_{k}u\cdot\nabla u^{j}\big)\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)-\mu(\rho,c)\right)\dvg u\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}G(\rho,c)\big(\dvg u\dvg\dot{u}-\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\big)-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\dvg\dot{u}.

The computation is similar to that of the one-phase model (see e.g.
[
43
, Appendix A]
), and we do not repeat it in this paper. The last term is

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\dvg\dot{u}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{2}P(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{3}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\nabla u^{j}\cdot\partial_{j}u.

By combining the above expressions and multiplying by
\kappa_{2}(t^{\prime})=\sigma(t^{\prime})\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}
we obtain (since
\kappa_{2}(0)=0
):

\displaystyle\dfrac{\kappa_{2}(t)}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\Big(\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)(t,x)dx+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\kappa_{2}(t^{\prime})\Big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}

(3.68)

\displaystyle\leqslant\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{2}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\Big(\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{2}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big).

In the following items, we estimate the terms on the RHS above.

\diamond

Since
\kappa_{2}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\leqslant C\langle t^{\prime}\rangle^{\frac{1+3r_{0}}{2}}\leqslant C\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})
, it follows that the first term on the RHS of (
3.68
) can be estimated as follows:

(3.69)

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{2}^{\prime}(t^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\Big(\left\lvert\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}\Big)(t^{\prime},x)dxdt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big).

\diamond

Multiplying (
3.50
) by a time weight
\check{\kappa}
, and integrating in time yields

\displaystyle\check{\kappa}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)(t,x)dx

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{(l+1)\mu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\leqslant\check{\kappa}(0)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho_{0},c_{0})(x)dx+\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{(l+1)\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big).

Combining this with (
3.63
), we obtain

\displaystyle\check{\kappa}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}

\displaystyle H_{l}(\rho,c)(t)+\dfrac{1}{(l+1)\mu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{l+1}_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\mu_{*}^{l+1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant\check{\kappa}(0)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho_{0},c_{0})(x)dx+C\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{l}}{(l+1)\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{l}}{(l+1)\mu_{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

Applying the commutator estimate (
A.8
) with
\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha/2
, and
p=l+1
, we obtain the following bound for the last term above:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{l}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}^{l+1}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}+C_{l}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{l+1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime},

where
\frac{1}{r}=\frac{\alpha}{6}+\frac{1}{l+1}
. Hence, under the smallness of the shear viscosity jump

C_{l}\frac{\mu^{*}}{\mu_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\mu_{*}}\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Bigg)^{l+1}\leqslant\frac{1}{2(l+1)},

we have

\displaystyle\check{\kappa}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{l+1}_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*,l}\Bigg(\check{\kappa}(0)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho_{0},c_{0})(x)dx+\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)+\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{l+1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)

(3.70)

\displaystyle+C_{*,l}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

Next, we interpolate

\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{l+1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{1-\frac{\alpha(l+1)}{3(l-1)}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}(l-1)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{\alpha(l+1)}{3(l-1)}};

and we express

\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)=\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)+\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c).

By iterating (
3.64
), it is straightforward to obtain
H_{l}(\rho,c)\leqslant C_{*,l}H_{1}(\rho,c)
and therefore

\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)\leqslant C_{*,l}C_{0}\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\check{\kappa}^{\prime},

while for the second term, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\check{\kappa}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{\eta}\Bigg(C_{*,l}\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\big(\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\check{\kappa}^{-\eta}\big)^{\frac{1}{1-\eta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1-\eta},\;\text{ for }\;\eta\in(0,1).

We now refer back to (

⋄

‣
3.4.3
), from which Young’s inequality provides

\displaystyle\check{\kappa}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{l}(\rho,c)(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{l+1}_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*,l}\Bigg[C_{0}\Bigg(\check{\kappa}(0)+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\Bigg)+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}(l-1)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}dt^{\prime}+\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\big(\check{\kappa}^{\prime}\check{\kappa}^{-\eta}\big)^{\frac{1}{1-\eta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c)\Bigg]

(3.71)

\displaystyle+C_{*,l}\int_{0}^{t}\check{\kappa}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{l+1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{l+1}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}.

In particular for
l=3
, and
\check{\kappa}=\kappa_{3}=\sigma(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2(1+r_{0})}
, we infer

\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}dt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}C_{0}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t),

and for
\eta=\frac{1+r_{0}}{2+r_{0}}
,

\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\big(\kappa_{3}^{\prime}\kappa_{3}^{-\eta}\big)^{\frac{1}{1-\eta}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c)\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{r_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{1}(\rho,c)dt^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}.

Next, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\sup_{[0,t]}\bigg[\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\bigg]\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)^{1/2}\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t).

From these, we deduce the following bound for the next-to-last term in (
3.68
):

\displaystyle\kappa_{3}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{3}(\rho,c)(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

(3.72)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\bigg(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)^{1/2}\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\bigg).

To estimate the last term, we start with

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{3}{4}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma(t^{\prime})^{2}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{3(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}

(3.73)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}^{3/4}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma(t^{\prime})^{2}\left\langle t^{\prime}\right\rangle^{3(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}dt^{\prime}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

In (

⋄

‣
3.4.3
), we take
l=5
,

\check{\kappa}=\kappa_{4}=\sigma^{2}\left\langle\right\rangle^{3(1+r_{0})}
to obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{4}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}

\displaystyle H_{5}(\rho,c)(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{4}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{6}_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{12}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{4}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u},\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{12}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big)+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{4}(t^{\prime})\left\lVert\rho\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}dt^{\prime}

(3.74)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{12}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big)+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)^{2}.

Finally, we combine (
3.74
)-(
3.73
)-(
3.72
)-(
3.69
)-(
3.68
) and we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)+\sup_{[0,t]}\kappa_{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{3}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big)(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\Big(1+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\Big)+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\Big(1+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}C_{0}\Big).

This completes the proof of (
2.23
).
∎

3.4.4.
Bound for the functional
\mathcal{A}_{3}

This section aims at obtaining bound for the energy functional
\mathcal{A}_{3}
.

Proof.

To obtain the functional
\mathcal{A}_{3}
, we first apply the material derivative
\partial_{t}\,\cdot+\dvg(\,\cdot\,u)
to the momentum equations (
2.1
) and obtain

(3.75)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\rho\dot{u}^{j})+\dvg(\rho\dot{u}^{j}u)=\partial_{k}\{\dot{\Pi}^{jk}\}+\partial_{k}\{\Pi^{jk}\dvg u\}-\dvg\{\partial_{k}u\Pi^{jk}\},

where

\displaystyle\dot{\Pi}^{jk}

\displaystyle=2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\dot{u}-\mu(\rho,c)\partial_{j}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{k}-\mu(\rho,c)\partial_{k}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{j}-2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\dvg u

\displaystyle+\left(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}-\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{l}\cdot\partial_{l}u-\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}+\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\right)\delta^{jk}.

We use
\kappa_{5}\ddot{u}
, with
\kappa_{5}=\sigma^{1+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}
as a test function to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\ddot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\kappa_{5}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\big)(t,x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\big)-\kappa_{5}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}\dvg u

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}\dvg u+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}

(3.76)

\displaystyle+\kappa_{5}(t)J_{1}(t)-\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}J_{1}(s)ds+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}J_{2}(s)ds+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}J_{3}(s)ds.

Above,
J_{1},\;J_{2},\;J_{3}
are of the form

(3.77)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}J_{1}&=\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}G(\rho,c),\\
J_{2}&=\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}\partial_{j_{7}}u^{j_{8}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}G(\rho,c)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}},\\
J_{3}&=\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}\dot{u}^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}\dot{u}^{j_{4}}G(\rho,c),\end{cases}

where
\phi
is either the viscosities
\mu
,
\lambda
or
\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu
,
\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda
,
\rho^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{2}\mu
or
\rho^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{2}\lambda
, while
\psi
corresponds to either
\rho\partial_{\rho}P
or
\rho^{2}\partial_{\rho}^{2}P
. The computations are analogous to those in
[
43
, Section B.2]
and we do not repeat it here. In the following steps, we provide estimates for the RHS terms of (
3.76
).

Step 1

In this paragraph, we will estimate the first term of the RHS of (
3.76
). For
\check{\alpha}=1
, it is clear that the underlying term is controlled as follows

\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\big)\leqslant C\mathcal{A}_{2}(t).

Even if
\check{\alpha}\in[0,1)
, we still have

\frac{1}{2}\int_{\sigma(t)}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\big)\leqslant C\mathcal{A}_{2}(t),

and to address the case of small time, we shall make use of a duality method. Hence, we write
\dot{u}=v+\check{v}
where
v
and
\check{v}
satisfy:

(3.78)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}(\rho v)+\dvg(\rho v\otimes u)=\dvg\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}v\big)+\nabla\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg v\big),\\
(\rho v)_{|t=0}=(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0},\end{cases}

and

(3.79)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}(\rho\check{v})+\dvg(\rho\check{v}\otimes u)-\dvg\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\check{v}\big)-\nabla\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg\check{v}\big)=\dvg(\mathscr{R}),\\
(\rho\check{v})_{|t=0}=0,\end{cases}

with

\displaystyle(\dvg\mathscr{R})^{j}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{k}\big(\mu(\rho,c)\partial_{j}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{k}+\mu(\rho,c)\partial_{k}u^{l}\partial_{l}u^{j}+2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}^{jk}u\dvg u\big)

\displaystyle-\partial_{j}\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{l}\cdot\partial_{l}u+\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}-\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\big)+\partial_{k}\big(\Pi^{jk}\dvg u\big)-\dvg\big(\partial_{k}u\Pi^{jk}\big).

Recalling that
(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
(see (
2.2
)), one can establish the existence and uniqueness of
v
, and thus of
\check{v}
, through a regularizing argument. We now aim at deriving bounds for
v
and
\check{v}
, which will then imply the desired bound for
\dot{u}
.

For
\tau\in(0,\sigma(t)]
, and
\check{\varphi}\in L^{2}((0,\tau)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we consider
the linear nonhomogeneous problem

(3.80)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\rho\partial_{t}w+\rho(u\cdot\nabla w)+\dvg(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}w)+\nabla(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg w)=\rho\check{\varphi},\\
w_{|t=\tau}=0.\end{cases}

The existence of
w
can be established by reversing the arrow of time, and we readily have (recall that
\tau\in[0,1]
)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sup_{[0,\tau]}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\bigg[2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}w\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg w)^{2}\Big]\leqslant C\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Next, using
\dot{w}
as a test function, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{w}\right\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big[2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}w\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)\left\lvert\dvg w\right\rvert^{2}\big](\tau^{\prime},x)dx

(3.81)

\displaystyle\leqslant\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{w}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Repeating the computations that led to (
2.6
), we find

\displaystyle\nabla w

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mu(\rho,c)}\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\mathbb{P}\big(\rho(\dot{w}-\check{\varphi})\big)+\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\bigg(\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\big(\rho(\dot{w}-\check{\varphi})\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu(\rho,c)}\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}w)-\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\bigg(\frac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}w)\bigg);

and by means of the commutator estimate (
A.8
), it follows, under the assumption of a small viscosity jump (see (
3.66
))

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\big(\rho(\dot{w}-\check{\varphi})\big)\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{1/2}\left\lVert\rho(\dot{w}-\check{\varphi})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{1/2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big).

Therefore, by Young’s inequality, (
3.81
) leads to:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{w}\right\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big[2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}w\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)\left\lvert\dvg w\right\rvert^{2}\big](\tau^{\prime},x)dx

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2},

from which Grönwall’s Lemma yields

(3.82)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{w}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\sup_{[0,\tau]}\left\lVert\nabla w\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant\Bigg(C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

We now use
w
as a test function in (
3.78
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\rho v)(\tau^{\prime},x)\check{\varphi}(\tau^{\prime},x)d\tau^{\prime}dx\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\rho v)_{|t=0}(x)w(0,x)dx\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\lVert(\rho v)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert w(0)\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(\rho v)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}((0,\tau)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

By duality, it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\rho v(\tau^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(\rho v)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]},

and using
\sigma v
as a testing function in (
3.78
) yields

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,\tau]}\sigma\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma\left\lVert\nabla v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(\rho v)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

As well, by using
v
as a test function in (
3.78
) it is straightforward to obtain

\sup_{[0,\tau]}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\left\lVert\nabla v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(\rho v)_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Finally, we apply a Riesz-Thorin theorem to conclude that

(3.83)

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,\tau]}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla v\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-\check{\alpha}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

This ends the estimate for
v
and we now turn to the one of
\check{v}
.

To achieve this, we rather consider the following Cauchy problem

(3.84)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\rho\partial_{t}\check{w}+\rho(u\cdot\nabla)\check{w}+\dvg\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\check{w}\big)+\nabla\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg\check{w}\big)=0,\\
\check{w}_{|t=\tau}=\check{\varphi},\end{cases}

where
0<\tau\leqslant\sigma(t)
and
\check{\varphi}=\check{\varphi}(x)\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
is a given function. A basic energy estimate yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sup_{[0,\tau]}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{w}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left[2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\check{w}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\check{w})^{2}\right]\leqslant\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}(\tau)\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Let
\sigma_{\tau}
denotes the time weight
\sigma_{\tau}=\tau-s
,
s\in[0,\tau]
. Using
\sigma_{\tau}\dot{\check{w}}
as a test function in (
3.84
) and following the same computations that lead to (
3.82
), we infer

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma_{\tau}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\check{w}}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\sup_{[0,\tau]}\sigma_{\tau}\left\lVert\nabla\check{w}\right\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}(\tau)\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

We now use
\check{w}
as a test function in (
3.79
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\rho\check{v})(\tau,x)\check{\varphi}(x)dx\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathscr{R}^{jk}(\tau^{\prime},x)\partial_{k}\check{w}^{j}(\tau^{\prime},x)dxd\tau^{\prime}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\bigg)(\tau^{\prime})\left\lVert\nabla\check{w}(\tau^{\prime})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}(\tau)\check{\varphi}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg[E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma_{\tau}^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

Hence, by duality we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{v}(\tau)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg[E_{0}^{\tfrac{1}{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma_{\tau}^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg]\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

Note that for all
r\in\big(0,4/(2+\check{\alpha})\big)
,
we have

\left\lVert\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right\rVert_{L^{r}(0,\sigma(t))}^{2}\leqslant C_{r}\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4},

and Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev’s inequality (see
[
1
, Theorem 1.7]
) yields

\left\lVert\tau\mapsto\int_{0}^{\tau}\sigma_{\tau}^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\left(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)\right\rVert_{L^{2r/(2-r)}(0,\sigma(t))}^{2}\leqslant C\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

By Hölder’s inequality, it follows that for
r\in\big(2/(1+\check{\alpha}),4/(2+\check{\alpha})\big)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\tau^{\check{\alpha}-1}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{v}(\tau)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}d\tau\leqslant C_{r}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{v}\right\rVert_{L^{2r/(2-r)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*,r}\Bigg(E_{0}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

We now use
\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\check{v}
as a test function in (
3.79
)
and we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,\sigma(t)]}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{v}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\check{v}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(E_{0}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}-1}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\check{v}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Bigg)

(3.85)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(E_{0}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

Finally, from (
3.83
)-(
3.85
) and
\dot{u}=v+\check{v}
,
we obtain that

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,\sigma(t)]}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(E_{0}+\left\lVert(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-\check{\alpha}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

Following the computations that go from (

⋄

‣
3.4.3
) to (
3.72
), we obtain

(3.86)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\tau^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\bigg(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big)\sup_{[0,\sigma(t)]}\sigma^{\frac{\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\bigg),

and Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,\sigma(t)]}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\left\lVert(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-\check{\alpha}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)\Bigg)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]},

and we conclude this step with the following bound for the first term on the RHS of (
3.76
)

\displaystyle\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)

\displaystyle:=\sup_{[0,t]}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\left\lvert\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\right\rvert^{2}+\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)+C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\left\lVert(\rho\dot{u})_{|t=0}\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{-\check{\alpha}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)\Bigg)

(3.87)

\displaystyle\times\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]}.

The others terms are estimated in the following steps.

Step 2

In what follows, we estimate all the remaining RHS terms in (
3.76
),
except for the last one, which is addressed next.

\diamond

We readily bound the second term on the RHS of (
3.76
) as follows:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\kappa_{5}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}\dvg u\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\kappa_{5}t)\left\lVert\dvg\dot{u}(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\left\lVert\dvg u(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\kappa_{5}t)\left\lVert\dvg\dot{u}(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\mathcal{A}_{2}(t).

The two terms that follow are

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}\dvg u\Bigg|+\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)(\dvg\dot{u})^{2}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(E_{0}+\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\Big).

\diamond

Next, using (
3.72
)-(
3.86
), we estimate the sixth and final terms in the expression of
J_{2}
(see (
3.77
)) as follows:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}^{\prime}J_{1}(s)ds\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\psi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\bigg(\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)+C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\bigg).

Following the computations leading to (
3.74
), we estimate the remaining terms of
J_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\phi(\rho,c)\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}\partial_{j_{7}}u^{j_{8}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j_{1}}\dot{u}^{j_{2}}\partial_{j_{3}}u^{j_{4}}\partial_{j_{5}}u^{j_{6}}G(\rho,c)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}\Big)

(3.88)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{12}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big)+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)\big(1+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\big).

\diamond

For the first term in the last line of (
3.76
), we have
we estimate

\left\lvert\kappa_{5}(t)J_{1}(t)\right\rvert\leqslant\eta\kappa_{5}(t)\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\kappa_{5}(t)\left\lVert\nabla u(t),G(\rho,c)(t)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}.

From (
3.47
), we have
\lvert G(\rho,c)\rvert^{4}\leqslant C_{*}H_{3}(\rho,c)
; and (
3.72
) yields:

\sigma(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)(t)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\leqslant C_{*}\bigg(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)^{1/2}\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\bigg).

From (
3.63
) and the commutator estimate (
A.8
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\Big(\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\Big(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{12/(2\alpha+3)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

and, using the smallness of the shear viscosity jump together with
interpolation and Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u}),\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C_{*}\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{2\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Then, Gagliardo–Nirenberg’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{11}{4}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big[\sigma\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big]\Big[\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big),

and hence

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,t]}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\big(C_{0}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)\big)

(3.89)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)+\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)+\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\big)\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big).

Finally, we have the following estimate for the first term in the last line of (
3.76
):

\displaystyle\left\lvert\kappa_{5}(t)J_{1}(t)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leqslant\eta\kappa_{5}(t)\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}(t)\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{*}}{\eta}\Big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)+\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)+\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\big)\big(C_{0}^{1/2}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{1/2}\big)\Big).

The remaining term will be addressed in the following item.

Step 3

We estimate the remaining term in (
3.76
) as follows:

\left\lvert\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}J_{3}(s)ds\right\rvert\leqslant C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)+C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Bigg)^{2/3}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\Bigg)^{1/3}.

We now express
\nabla\dot{u}
in order to estimate its
L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
-norm. To achieve this, we apply to (
3.75
) the computations that lead to (
2.6
). Indeed, applying the Leray projector
\mathbb{P}
to (
3.75
), we obtain

\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u})=\dvg\mathbb{P}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\big)+\dvg\mathbb{P}\bigg(\big(\Pi-2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}u\big)\dvg u-\Pi\cdot\nabla u-\mu(\rho,c)\big(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u)^{t}\big)\bigg)

and since

\displaystyle\dvg\mathbb{P}\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\big)

\displaystyle=\dvg\mathbb{P}\big(\big(\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big)(2\mathbb{D}\dot{u})\big)+\widetilde{\mu}\Delta\mathbb{P}\dot{u},

it follows that

\displaystyle\mu(\rho,c)\nabla\mathbb{P}\dot{u}

\displaystyle=-(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u})+\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}\dot{u})

(3.90)

\displaystyle+\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\mathbb{P}\bigg(\big(\Pi-2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}u\big)\dvg u-\Pi\cdot\nabla u-\mu(\rho,c)\big(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u)^{t}\big)\bigg).

Next, applying the divergence operator to (
3.75
), we obtain

\displaystyle\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})

\displaystyle=\dvg\dvg\big(2\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}\dot{u}\big)+\Delta\big(\lambda(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}-\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{l}\cdot\partial_{l}u-\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}+\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u\big)

\displaystyle+\dvg\dvg\bigg(\big(\Pi-2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}u\big)\dvg u-\Pi\cdot\nabla u-\mu(\rho,c)\big(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u)^{t}\big)\bigg),

and therefore,
\check{F}
given by

\check{F}=\nu(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}-\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{l}\cdot\partial_{l}u-\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}+\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u

can be expressed as

\displaystyle\check{F}

\displaystyle=-(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})+\big[\mathcal{K}^{\prime},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u)

(3.91)

\displaystyle+(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg\dvg\bigg(\big(\Pi-2\rho\partial_{\rho}\mu(\rho,c)\mathbb{D}u\big)\dvg u-\Pi\cdot\nabla u-\mu(\rho,c)\big(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u+(\nabla u\cdot\nabla u)^{t}\big)\bigg).

In turn, we can express
\dvg\dot{u}
as

(3.92)

\displaystyle\nu(\rho,c)\dvg\dot{u}=\check{F}+\lambda(\rho,c)\nabla u^{l}\cdot\partial_{l}u+\rho\partial_{\rho}\lambda(\rho,c)(\dvg u)^{2}-\rho\partial_{\rho}P(\rho,c)\dvg u.

From (
3.92
)-(
3.4.4
) it follows that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\nabla\mathbb{P}\dot{u}-\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\dvg\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C_{*}\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\mu_{*}}\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K}^{\prime},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}u),\,\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big](2\mathbb{D}\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},

and the commutator estimate (
A.8
), together with the smallness of the shear viscosity jump yields

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(3.93)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big),

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{5}\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\ddot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\bigg)^{3/4}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{2+3\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}\Bigg)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big).

With the help of the expressions of
(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u})
and
(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})
in (
3.4.4
)-(
3.91
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{2+3\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{2+3\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}+\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{12}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(C_{0}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\Big[\sup_{[0,t]}\sigma^{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{3\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big]^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Bigg).

It follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)^{3/4}\Big(C_{0}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)^{2}\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\Big)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)^{3/4}\Big[\sup_{[0,t]}\sigma^{\frac{3}{4}+\frac{3\check{\alpha}}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Big]^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\Bigg)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\big),

and we recall the estimates (
3.89
)-(
3.72
)-(
3.73
)-(
3.74
).

Summing all the estimates above and applying Young’s inequality, we conclude that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{5}{4}+\frac{3}{2}\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(\check{C}_{0}+\mathcal{L}\bigg(\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}C_{0},\,\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\bigg)\Big)\exp{\Bigg[C_{*}\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big)\Bigg]},

where
\mathcal{L}
is a polynomial, whose exact expression is not needed for the remaining steps in the proof of Theorem
1.2
. Estimate (
2.24
) finally follows from (see (
3.4.2
))

\int_{0}^{\sigma(t)}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{3}\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}E_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)+C_{*}E_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{\frac{5}{4}}+C_{0}^{\frac{5}{4}}\big).

∎

3.5.
Proof of Lemma
2.4

Proof.

From(
3.4.4
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{1+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}(1+r_{0})}\left\lVert\rho\ddot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\ddot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\ddot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{3}(t)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)\Bigg)^{1/2}

(3.94)

\displaystyle+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big).

Using (
3.86
), we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\Big(\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}\Big(\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)+C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{6}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)+\big(C_{0}+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\big)\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\Big),

and with the help of (
3.88
), the remaining terms can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{\frac{1}{2}+\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{1+2\check{\alpha}}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\big(\left\lVert\nabla u,\,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{6}+\left\lVert\dvg u\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)\Bigg)^{1/2}+C_{*}\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}\left\lVert\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(C_{*}C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{12}{\alpha}}\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)^{2}\big)+C_{*}\mathcal{A}_{2}(t)\big(1+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t)\big)\Bigg)^{1/2}+C_{*}\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\check{\mathcal{A}}_{2}(t).

The
L^{2}((0,t),L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))
-norm of
\left\langle\right\rangle^{(1+r_{0})/2}\big(\nabla u,G(\rho,c)\big)
will be estimated in what follows.

To achieve this, we first deduce from (
3.48
)-(
3.49
) to obtain

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dfrac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\left\lvert G(\rho,c)\right\rvert^{6}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dfrac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}FG(\rho,c)\left\lvert G(\rho,c)\right\rvert^{4},

and from (
3.46
)-(
3.47
), we have

(3.95)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\rho}(c)g(\rho,c)H_{l}(\rho,c)\leqslant\left\lvert G(\rho,c)\right\rvert^{l+1}\leqslant\frac{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{a_{l}(c)}g(\rho,c)H_{l}(\rho,c),\;\text{ where }\;g(\rho,c)=\frac{G(\rho,c)}{\rho-\widetilde{\rho}(c)}.

We will use the following notations:

(3.96)

\displaystyle a_{l,*}=\inf_{c}a_{l}(c),\;\;g^{*}=\sup_{\rho,c}g(\rho,c),\;\;g_{*}=\inf_{\rho,c}g(\rho,c),\;\;k_{*}=\dfrac{\rho_{*}g_{*}}{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}\;\text{ and }\;k^{*}_{l}=\frac{\nu^{*}}{a_{l,*}\nu_{*}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\rho}(c)\dfrac{g(\rho,c)}{\nu(\rho,c)}H_{5}(\rho,c)

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\dfrac{1}{\nu(\rho,c)}\left\lvert F\right\rvert^{6}\Bigg)^{1/6}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\rho+\widetilde{\rho}(c)}{a_{5}(c)}\frac{g(\rho,c)}{\nu(\rho,c)}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{5/6}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{1}{\nu_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}{a_{5,*}}\Bigg)^{5/6}\left\lVert F\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{5/6}.

Hence

(3.97)

\displaystyle 3\dfrac{d}{dt}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}+\dfrac{\rho_{*}g_{*}}{\nu^{*}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}\leqslant\frac{1}{\nu_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}{a_{5,*}}\Bigg)^{5/6}\left\lVert F\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/6},

and Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle 3\dfrac{d}{dt}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}+\dfrac{\rho_{*}g_{*}}{2\nu^{*}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}\leqslant\frac{\nu^{*}\nu_{*}^{-2}}{2\rho_{*}g_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}{a_{5,*}}\Bigg)^{5/3}\left\lVert F\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Upon multiplying by the time weight
\kappa_{1}=\left\langle\right\rangle^{1+r_{0}}
and integrating in time, we obtain:

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(t)\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)(t)\Bigg)^{1/3}+\dfrac{\rho_{*}g_{*}}{6\nu^{*}}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho_{0},c_{0})\Bigg)^{1/3}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}

\displaystyle+\frac{\nu^{*}\nu_{*}^{-2}}{6\rho_{*}g_{*}}\Bigg(\frac{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}{a_{5,*}}\Bigg)^{5/3}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert F\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

From (
3.64
)-(
3.52
) we infer

\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho_{0},c_{0})\Bigg)^{1/3}+\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}^{\prime}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}\leqslant C_{*}C_{0},

and (
3.95
), yields

\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}H_{5}(\rho,c)\Bigg)^{1/3}\geqslant\Big(\frac{a_{5,*}}{2\rho^{*}g^{*}}\Big)^{1/3}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle k_{*}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}+{k_{5}^{*}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert F\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Once again, we combine (
3.63
)–(
A.8
) to deduce that

\displaystyle k_{*}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\Big(\left\lVert G(\rho,c)\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\mu_{*}^{2}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\mathbb{P}(\rho\dot{u}),\,(-\Delta)^{-1}\dvg(\rho\dot{u})\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(k_{*}^{2}+{k_{5}^{*}}^{2}\big)\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\big[\mathcal{K},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u,\,\big[\check{\mathcal{K}},\mu(\rho,c)-\widetilde{\mu}\big]\mathbb{D}u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant C_{*}C_{0}+C_{*}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\Big(\left\lVert\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{2}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6/(1+\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+C\big(k_{*}^{2}+{k_{5}^{*}}^{2}\big)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

Under the smallness assumption on the shear-viscosity jump

\frac{C}{\mu_{*}^{2}}\Bigg(1+\frac{{k_{5}^{*}}^{2}}{k_{*}^{2}}\Bigg)\sup_{[0,t]}\left\lVert\llbracket\mu(\rho,c)\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}^{2}\leqslant\frac{1}{2},

the last term can be absorbed into the LHS, and Young’s inequality gives

\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert G(\rho,c),\,\nabla u\right\rVert_{L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leqslant C_{*}\Big(C_{0}\big(1+\check{\phi}_{\Sigma}^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}\big)+\mathcal{A}_{1}(t)\Big).

This completes the estimate of the last term in (
3.94
).

Using an interpolation inequality, we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{6/(2-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C_{*}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1-\alpha}{4}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\kappa_{1}\left\lVert\nabla\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1+\alpha}{4}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\kappa^{-1}_{1}\sigma^{-\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and

\diamond

if
\alpha\in(0,1/2)
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left\lVert\dot{u}\right\rVert_{L^{3/(1-\alpha)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}
