\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)\big(w^{e}(z)-w^{e}(x)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\int_{B(y,2\delta)}K(y-z)\big(w^{e}(z)-w^{e}(y)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}.

To estimate the third term on the RHS of (
A.38
), we express

(A.39)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}g(x)-\int_{D\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz-\int_{D^{c}\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz,

and we estimate:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)\big(g(z)-g(x)\big)dz\Bigg|+\left\lvert g(x)\right\rvert\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)dz\Bigg|

(A.40)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\alpha^{\prime}}+C\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},

where we make use of the extra cancellation of even-order Riesz transform (see
[
2
,
36
]
) to bound the last term of the RHS of the first line. For the last term of (
A.39
), we express

\int_{D^{c}\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz=\int_{D^{c}\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)\big(g(z)-g(\check{x})\big)dz+g(\check{x})\int_{D^{c}\cap B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)dz,

where
\check{x}\in\partial D
is such that
d(x,\partial D)=\left\lvert x-\check{x}\right\rvert
. We observe that that the estimate (
A.40
) also holds for the last term of (
A.39
) and we obtain:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\big(w^{e}(y)-w^{e}(x)\big)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g,\,\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Bigg)\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}.

To estimate the last term of (
A.38
), we observe that

\displaystyle\left\lvert K(x-z)-K(y-z)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert\int_{0}^{1}\left\lvert\nabla K\big(\tau x+(1-\tau)y-z\big)\right\rvert d\tau

\displaystyle\leqslant C\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert}{\left\lvert\tau x+(1-\tau)y-z\right\rvert^{4}}d\tau.

Hence, for all
z\notin B(y,2\delta)
, and
\tau\in(0,1)
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\tau x+(1-\tau)y-z\right\rvert

\displaystyle\geqslant\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert-\tau\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert

\displaystyle\geqslant\frac{1}{2}\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert+\Big(\frac{1}{2}\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert-\tau\delta\Big)

\displaystyle\geqslant\frac{1}{2}\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert+\Big(\frac{1}{2}(2\delta)-\tau\delta\Big)

\displaystyle\geqslant\frac{1}{2}\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert.

In consequence, we have:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus B(y,2\delta)}

\displaystyle\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)\big(w^{e}(z)-w^{e}(y)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus B(y,2\delta)}\left\lvert y-z\right\rvert^{\beta-4}dz

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}.

We finally obtain:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\big[\mathcal{T},w^{e}\big]g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\big(\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g,\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert w\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell^{-\beta}_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert g,\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(A.41)

\displaystyle+C\Big(\left\lvert w\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta-\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell^{-\beta+\alpha^{\prime}}_{\Sigma}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},

where we make use of (
A.29
).

Using (
A.34
) we express

\displaystyle\big[\mathcal{T},(w-w^{e})\big]g(x)

\displaystyle-\big[\mathcal{T},(w-w^{e})\big]g(y)

\displaystyle=\int_{D\cap B(x,\delta)}K(x-z)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(x)\big)g(z)dz

\displaystyle-\int_{D\cap B(x,\delta)}K(y-z)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(y)\big)g(z)dz

\displaystyle+\int_{D\cap B^{c}(x,\delta)}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(x)\big)g(z)dz

\displaystyle+\big((w-w^{e})(y)-(w-w^{e})(x)\big)\Bigg(\int_{D\cap B^{c}(x,\delta)}K(y-z)g(z)dz+\int_{D^{c}}K(y-z)g(z)dz\Bigg)

(A.42)

\displaystyle-(w-w^{e})(x)\int_{D^{c}}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)g(z)dz.

As in the previous step, we estimate the first three terms on the RHS as follows:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B(x,\delta)}K(x-z)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(x)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|+\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B(x,\delta)}K(y-z)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(y)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B^{c}(x,\delta)}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)\big((w-w^{e})(z)-(w-w^{e})(x)\big)g(z)dz\Bigg|\leqslant C\left\lvert w-w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}.

To bound the last term, we express:

\displaystyle\int_{D^{c}}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)g(z)dz

\displaystyle=\int_{D^{c}\cap B(\check{x},2\delta)}K(x-z)\big(g(z)-g(\check{x})\big)dz-\int_{D^{c}\cap B(\check{x},2\delta)}K(y-z)\big(g(z)-g(\check{y})\big)dz

\displaystyle+\big(g(\check{y})-g(\check{x})\big)\int_{D\cap B(\check{x},2\delta)}K(y-z)dz+\int_{D^{c}\cap B^{c}(\check{x},2\delta)}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)\big(g(z)-g(\check{x})\big)dz

\displaystyle-g(\check{x})\int_{D}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)dz.

Following the preceding step, all terms on the RHS, except for the last, are bounded by

C\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(D^{c})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}.

Next, we apply Theorem 2.3 of
[
16
]
to estimate the last term as

\bigg|g(\check{x})\int_{D}\big(K(x-z)-K(y-z)\big)dz\Bigg|\leqslant C\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\beta}}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta},

where
\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}
is a polynomial in
\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert
,
\left\lVert\Sigma\right\rVert_{\text{Lip}}
and
\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\text{inf}}^{-1}
.
For the remaining term of (
A.42
), we express

\int_{D\cap B^{c}(x,\delta)}K(y-z)g(z)dz+\int_{D^{c}}K(y-z)g(z)dz=\mathcal{T}g-\int_{D\cap B(x,\delta)}K(y-z)g(z)dz,

and we estimate

\Bigg|\int_{D\cap B^{c}(x,\delta)}K(y-z)g(z)dz+\int_{D^{c}}K(y-z)g(z)dz\Bigg|\leqslant C\Big(\left\lVert g,\,\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}\Big).

In sum, we obtain:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\big[\mathcal{T},(w-w^{e})\big]g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(A.43)

\displaystyle+C\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\beta}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\beta}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\mathcal{T}g,g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Estimate (
3
) simply follows from (
A.41
)-(
A.43
). This completes the proof of Lemma
A.2
.

Let us observe that following the same computations as above, one obtains (
r\in[1,\infty)
and
\alpha^{\prime}\in(0,\alpha]
):

(A.44)

\left\lVert\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\Big(\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\frac{3}{r}}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{\mathscr{C}^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\Big),

and

(A.45)

\left\lVert\mathcal{T}g\right\rVert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leqslant C\Big(\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}_{pw,\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\alpha}}\Big).

Also, we may notice that in the case
\beta\in(0,\alpha)
, the third term on the RHS of (
A.38
) can be estimated as

\displaystyle\Bigg|\big(w^{e}(y)-w^{e}(x)\big)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus B(y,2\delta)}K(x-z)g(z)dz\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\alpha}\int_{\left\lvert x-z\right\rvert\geqslant 2\delta}\left\lvert x-z\right\rvert^{-3}\left\lvert g(z)\right\rvert dz

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lvert w^{e}\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{3/(\alpha-\beta)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\left\lvert x-y\right\rvert^{\beta}

leading to

\displaystyle\left\lvert\big[\mathcal{T},w\big]g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\left\lvert g\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}_{\text{pw},\Sigma}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+C\Big(\left\lvert w\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\ell_{\Sigma}^{-\alpha}\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert g\right\rVert_{L^{3/(\alpha-\beta)}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

(A.46)

\displaystyle+C\Big(\left\lvert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{\beta}(\Sigma)}+\big(\ell^{-\beta}_{\Sigma}+\mathfrak{P}_{\Sigma}\left\lvert\Sigma\right\rvert_{\dot{\mathscr{C}}^{1+\beta}}\big)\left\lVert\llbracket w\rrbracket\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\Big)\left\lVert\mathcal{T}g,g\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

∎
